+import { Controller, Get, Put, Param, Body, ForbiddenException } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { ApiTags, ApiOperation } from '@nestjs/swagger';
+import { AdminService } from './admin.service';
+import { GetUser } from '../auth/decorators/get-user.decorator';
+
+@ApiTags('Admin')
+@Controller('admin')
+export class AdminController {
+  constructor(private readonly adminService: AdminService) {}
+
+  @Get('users')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get all users' })
+  getUsers(@GetUser('role') role: string) {
+    if (role !== 'ADMIN') throw new ForbiddenException('Only ADMIN can view users');
+    return this.adminService.getUsers();
+  }
+
+  @Put('users/:id/role')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Set user role' })
+  setRole(@GetUser('role') callerRole: string, @Param('id') userId: string, @Body('role') newRole: string) {
+    if (callerRole !== 'ADMIN') throw new ForbiddenException('Only ADMIN can change roles');
+    return this.adminService.setRole(userId, newRole);
+  }
+}

+import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class AdminService {
+  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}
+
+  async getUsers() {
+    return this.prisma.user.findMany({
+      select: {
+        id: true,
+        email: true,
+        name: true,
+        role: true,
+        createdAt: true,
+      },
+      orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async setRole(userId: string, role: any) {
+    const user = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { id: userId } });
+    if (!user) throw new NotFoundException('User not found');
+    return this.prisma.user.update({
+      where: { id: userId },
+      data: { role },
+      select: { id: true, email: true, name: true, role: true },
+    });
+  }
+}

+import { Controller, Get, Post, Param, ForbiddenException } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { ApiTags, ApiOperation } from '@nestjs/swagger';
+import { NotificationsService } from './notifications.service';
+import { GetUser } from '../auth/decorators/get-user.decorator';
+
+@ApiTags('Notifications')
+@Controller('notifications')
+export class NotificationsController {
+  constructor(private readonly notificationsService: NotificationsService) {}
+
+  @Get()
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get all notifications for current user' })
+  getMyNotifications(@GetUser('sub') userId: string) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.notificationsService.getMyNotifications(userId);
+  }
+
+  @Post(':id/read')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Mark a notification as read' })
+  markAsRead(@GetUser('sub') userId: string, @Param('id') id: string) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.notificationsService.markAsRead(userId, id);
+  }
+
+  @Post('read-all')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Mark all notifications as read' })
+  markAllAsRead(@GetUser('sub') userId: string) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.notificationsService.markAllAsRead(userId);
+  }
+}

diff --git a/packages/backend/notifications/notifications.service.ts b/packages/backend/notifications/notifications.service.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..de316ea
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/backend/notifications/notifications.service.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class NotificationsService {
+  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}
+
+  async createNotification(userId: string, title: string, message: string, link?: string) {
+    return this.prisma.notification.create({
+      data: {
+        userId,
+        title,
+        message,
+        link,
+      },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async getMyNotifications(userId: string) {
+    return this.prisma.notification.findMany({
+      where: { userId },
+      orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async markAsRead(userId: string, id: string) {
+    const notification = await this.prisma.notification.findFirst({
+      where: { id, userId },
+    });
+    if (!notification) throw new NotFoundException('Notification not found');
+
+    return this.prisma.notification.update({
+      where: { id },
+      data: { isRead: true },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async markAllAsRead(userId: string) {
+    return this.prisma.notification.updateMany({
+      where: { userId, isRead: false },
+      data: { isRead: true },
+    });
+  }
+}

+import { IsEnum, IsNotEmpty, IsOptional, IsString } from 'class-validator';
+import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';
+
+export enum PaymentType {
+  PAID = 'PAID',
+  UNPAID = 'UNPAID',
+}
+
+export class CreateOpportunityDto {
+  @ApiProperty()
+  @IsString()
+  @IsNotEmpty()
+  title: string;
+
+  @ApiProperty()
+  @IsString()
+  @IsNotEmpty()
+  description: string;
+
+  @ApiProperty()
+  @IsString()
+  @IsNotEmpty()
+  ownerContactInfo: string;
+
+  @ApiProperty({ enum: PaymentType, default: PaymentType.UNPAID })
+  @IsEnum(PaymentType)
+  paymentType: PaymentType;
+
+  @ApiPropertyOptional()
+  @IsString()
+  @IsOptional()
+  paymentDetails?: string;

+import { Controller, Get, Post, Body, Param, Put, Query, ForbiddenException } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { ApiTags, ApiOperation } from '@nestjs/swagger';
+import { OpportunitiesService } from './opportunities.service';
+import { CreateOpportunityDto, UpdateOpportunityDto, ApplyOpportunityDto, ModerateOpportunityDto } from './dto';
+import { GetUser } from '../auth/decorators/get-user.decorator';
+import { Public } from '../auth/decorators/public.decorator';
+
+@ApiTags('Opportunities')
+@Controller('opportunities')
+export class OpportunitiesController {
+  constructor(private readonly opportunitiesService: OpportunitiesService) {}
+
+  @Post()
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Create a new opportunity' })
+  create(@GetUser('sub') userId: string, @Body() dto: CreateOpportunityDto) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.opportunitiesService.create(userId, dto);
+  }
+
+  @Public()
+  @Get()
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get all published opportunities (or filter)' })
+  findAll(@Query() query: any) {
+    return this.opportunitiesService.findAll(query);
+  }
+
+  @Get('my')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get current user opportunities' })
+  getMyOpportunities(@GetUser('sub') userId: string) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.opportunitiesService.getMyOpportunities(userId);
+  }
+
+  @Put('my/applications/:id/withdraw')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Applicant withdraws their application' })
+  withdrawApplication(@GetUser('sub') userId: string, @Param('id') applicationId: string) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.opportunitiesService.withdrawApplication(userId, applicationId);
+  }
+
+  @Get('my/applications')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get current user applications' })
+  getMyApplications(@GetUser('sub') userId: string) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.opportunitiesService.getMyApplications(userId);
+  }
+
+  @Public()
+  @Get(':id')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get opportunity by ID' })
+  findOne(@Param('id') id: string) {
+    return this.opportunitiesService.findOne(id);
+  }
+
+  @Put(':id')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Update your opportunity' })
+  update(@GetUser('sub') userId: string, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() dto: UpdateOpportunityDto) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.opportunitiesService.update(userId, id, dto);
+  }
+
+  @Post(':id/status')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Owner sends opportunity to review' })
+  changeStatus(@GetUser('sub') userId: string, @Param('id') id: string, @Body('status') status: 'READY_FOR_REVIEW') {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.opportunitiesService.changeStatus(userId, id, status);
+  }
+
+  @Put(':id/lifecycle')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Owner changes lifecycle state' })
+  changeLifecycleState(@GetUser('sub') userId: string, @Param('id') id: string, @Body('lifecycleState') lifecycleState: string) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.opportunitiesService.changeLifecycleState(userId, id, lifecycleState);
+  }
+
+  @Get(':id/applications')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Owner gets all applications for their opportunity' })
+  getOpportunityApplications(@GetUser('sub') userId: string, @Param('id') id: string) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.opportunitiesService.getOpportunityApplications(userId, id);
+  }
+
+  @Put('applications/:appId/status')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Owner updates application status' })
+  updateApplicationStatus(@GetUser('sub') userId: string, @Param('appId') appId: string, @Body() body: { status: string, comment?: string }) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.opportunitiesService.updateApplicationStatus(userId, appId, body.status, body.comment);
+  }
+
+  @Post(':id/apply')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Apply to an opportunity' })
+  apply(@GetUser('sub') userId: string, @Param('id') id: string, @Body() dto: ApplyOpportunityDto) {
+    if (!userId) throw new ForbiddenException('User not authenticated');
+    return this.opportunitiesService.apply(userId, id, dto);
+  }
+
+  @Post(':id/moderate')
+  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Moderate an opportunity (MODERATOR only)' })
+  moderate(@Param('id') id: string, @Body() dto: ModerateOpportunityDto) {
+    return this.opportunitiesService.moderate(id, dto);
+  }
+}

+import { OpportunitiesService } from './opportunities.service';
+import { OpportunitiesController } from './opportunities.controller';
+import { PrismaModule } from '../prisma/prisma.module';

diff --git a/packages/backend/opportunities/opportunities.service.ts b/packages/backend/opportunities/opportunities.service.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e099ab9
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/backend/opportunities/opportunities.service.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,207 @@
+import { Injectable, NotFoundException, ForbiddenException, BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
+import { NotificationsService } from '../notifications/notifications.service';
+import { CreateOpportunityDto, UpdateOpportunityDto, ApplyOpportunityDto, ModerateOpportunityDto, ModerateAction, PaymentType } from './dto';
+
+@Injectable()
+export class OpportunitiesService {
+  constructor(
+    private prisma: PrismaService,
+    private notificationsService: NotificationsService,
+  ) {}
+
+  async create(userId: string, dto: CreateOpportunityDto) {
+    return this.prisma.opportunity.create({
+      data: {
+        ...dto,
+        ownerId: userId,
+        status: 'DRAFT', // initial status
+      },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async findAll(query: any) {
+    const { status, paymentType, ownerId, search } = query;
+    const where: any = {};
+    if (status) where.status = status;
+    else where.status = 'PUBLISHED'; // Default to published for general list
+    if (paymentType) where.paymentType = paymentType;
+    if (ownerId) where.ownerId = ownerId;
+    if (search) {
+      where.OR = [
+        { title: { contains: search, mode: 'insensitive' } },
+        { description: { contains: search, mode: 'insensitive' } },
+      ];
+    }
+
+    return this.prisma.opportunity.findMany({
+      where,
+      orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
+      include: {
+        owner: {
+          select: { id: true, name: true, email: true },
+        },
+      },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async findOne(id: string) {
+    const opportunity = await this.prisma.opportunity.findUnique({
+      where: { id },
+      include: {
+        owner: { select: { id: true, name: true, email: true } },
+      },
+    });
+    if (!opportunity) throw new NotFoundException('Opportunity not found');
+    return opportunity;
+  }
+
+  async update(userId: string, id: string, dto: UpdateOpportunityDto) {
+    const opportunity = await this.findOne(id);
+    if (opportunity.ownerId !== userId) {
+      throw new ForbiddenException('You can only update your own opportunities');
+    }
+    return this.prisma.opportunity.update({
+      where: { id },
+      data: dto,
+    });
+  }
+
+  async changeStatus(userId: string, id: string, status: 'READY_FOR_REVIEW') {
+    const opportunity = await this.findOne(id);
+    if (opportunity.ownerId !== userId) throw new ForbiddenException('Not your opportunity');
+    return this.prisma.opportunity.update({
+      where: { id },
+      data: { status },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async changeLifecycleState(userId: string, id: string, lifecycleState: any) {
+    const opportunity = await this.findOne(id);
+    if (opportunity.ownerId !== userId) throw new ForbiddenException('Not your opportunity');
+    return this.prisma.opportunity.update({
+      where: { id },
+      data: { lifecycleState },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async getOpportunityApplications(userId: string, id: string) {
+    const opportunity = await this.findOne(id);
+    if (opportunity.ownerId !== userId) throw new ForbiddenException('Only the owner can view applications');
+    return this.prisma.opportunityApplication.findMany({
+      where: { opportunityId: id },
+      include: { applicant: { select: { name: true, email: true } } },
+      orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async updateApplicationStatus(userId: string, applicationId: string, status: any, ownerComment?: string) {
+    const app = await this.prisma.opportunityApplication.findUnique({
+      where: { id: applicationId },
+      include: { opportunity: true },
+    });
+    if (!app) throw new NotFoundException('Application not found');
+    if (app.opportunity.ownerId !== userId) throw new ForbiddenException('Only the owner can manage this');
+    
+    const updated = await this.prisma.opportunityApplication.update({
+      where: { id: applicationId },
+      data: { status, ownerComment },
+    });
+
+    let statusText = status;
+    if (status === 'ACCEPTED') statusText = 'принят';
+    if (status === 'REJECTED') statusText = 'отклонен';
+    if (status === 'UNDER_REVIEW') statusText = 'на рассмотрении';
+
+    await this.notificationsService.createNotification(
+      app.applicantId,
+      'Статус отклика изменен',
+      `Ваш отклик на "${app.opportunity.title}" теперь ${statusText}.${ownerComment ? ` Комментарий: ${ownerComment}` : ''}`,
+      `/my-applications`
+    );
+
+    return updated;
+  }
+
+  async apply(userId: string, id: string, dto: ApplyOpportunityDto) {
+    const opportunity = await this.findOne(id);
+    if (opportunity.status !== 'PUBLISHED') {
+      throw new BadRequestException('Cannot apply to an unpublished opportunity');
+    }
+    const existing = await this.prisma.opportunityApplication.findFirst({
+      where: { applicantId: userId, opportunityId: id },
+    });
+    if (existing) {
+      throw new BadRequestException('Already applied to this opportunity');
+    }
+    const app = await this.prisma.opportunityApplication.create({
+      data: {
+        ...dto,
+        applicantId: userId,
+        opportunityId: id,
+        status: 'SUBMITTED',
+      },
+    });
+
+    await this.notificationsService.createNotification(
+      opportunity.ownerId,
+      'Новый отклик!',
+      `Пользователь ${dto.applicantName} откликнулся на вашу возможность "${opportunity.title}".`,
+      `/my-opportunities/${id}`
+    );
+
+    return app;
+  }
+
+  
+  async withdrawApplication(userId: string, applicationId: string) {
+    const app = await this.prisma.opportunityApplication.findUnique({ where: { id: applicationId } });
+    if (!app) throw new NotFoundException('Application not found');
+    if (app.applicantId !== userId) throw new ForbiddenException('Only the applicant can withdraw');
+    if (app.status === 'ACCEPTED' || app.status === 'REJECTED') throw new BadRequestException('Cannot withdraw completed application');
+    return this.prisma.opportunityApplication.update({
+      where: { id: applicationId },
+      data: { status: 'WITHDRAWN' },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async getMyApplications(userId: string) {
+    return this.prisma.opportunityApplication.findMany({
+      where: { applicantId: userId },
+      include: { opportunity: true },
+      orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async getMyOpportunities(userId: string) {
+    return this.prisma.opportunity.findMany({
+      where: { ownerId: userId },
+      orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
+    });
+  }
+
+  async moderate(id: string, dto: ModerateOpportunityDto) {
+    let newStatus: any = 'DRAFT';
+    let actionText = '';
+    if (dto.action === ModerateAction.APPROVE) { newStatus = 'PUBLISHED'; actionText = 'одобрена'; }
+    else if (dto.action === ModerateAction.REJECT) { newStatus = 'REJECTED'; actionText = 'отклонена'; }
+    else if (dto.action === ModerateAction.REQUIRE_CHANGES) { newStatus = 'REQUIRES_CHANGES'; actionText = 'возвращена на правки'; }
+
+    const opp = await this.prisma.opportunity.update({
+      where: { id },
+      data: {
+        status: newStatus,
+        moderationComment: dto.comment,
+      },
+    });
+
+    await this.notificationsService.createNotification(
+      opp.ownerId,
+      `Возможность ${actionText}`,
+      `Ваша возможность "${opp.title}" была ${actionText} модератором.${dto.comment ? ` Комментарий: ${dto.comment}` : ''}`,
+      `/my-opportunities`
+    );
+
+    return opp;
+  }
+}

+import { PrismaClient } from '@universe/database';
+import { PrismaPg } from '@prisma/adapter-pg';
+import { Pool } from 'pg';
+
+const connectionString = process.env.DATABASE_URL || 'postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/postgres';
+const pool = new Pool({ connectionString });
+const adapter = new PrismaPg(pool);
+const prisma = new PrismaClient({ adapter });
+
+async function main() {
+  const users = await prisma.user.findMany({ take: 2 });
+  if (users.length === 0) {
+    console.log('No users in DB, creating a dummy user');
+    const user = await prisma.user.create({
+      data: {
+        email: 'test@karazin.ua',
+        name: 'Test Owner',
+        password: 'hash',
+        role: 'STUDENT'
+      }
+    });
+    users.push(user);
+  }
+
+  const owner = users[0];
+
+  await prisma.opportunity.createMany({
+    data: [
+      {
+        title: 'Frontend Developer for Edu Startup',
+        description: 'Ищем React разработчика для участия в разработке стартапа для студентов. Нужно знать React, Next.js, TypeScript.',

+        status: 'PUBLISHED',
+        lifecycleState: 'ACTIVE',
+        paymentType: 'PAID',
+        paymentDetails: '500$ в месяц',
+        ownerId: owner.id,
+      },
+      {
+        title: 'Data Science Intern',
+        description: 'Лаборатория машинного обучения приглашает на практику. Будем анализировать данные. Python, Pandas, Scikit-learn.',
+        ownerContactInfo: 'ds_lab@karazin.ua',
+        status: 'PUBLISHED',
+        lifecycleState: 'START',
+        paymentType: 'UNPAID',
+        ownerId: owner.id,
+      },
+      {
+        title: 'SMM-менеджер для проекта',
+        description: 'Нужен человек для ведения Instagram и Telegram канала студенческого совета.',
+        ownerContactInfo: '@student_council',
+        status: 'PUBLISHED',
+        lifecycleState: 'ACTIVE',
+        paymentType: 'UNPAID',
+        ownerId: owner.id,
+      }
+    ]
+  });
+
+  console.log('Seeded 3 opportunities');
+}

+main().catch(console.error).finally(() => prisma.$disconnect());

+# Opportunities Module
+
+This is the Next.js client for the Opportunities board. It integrates with the NestJS backend via proxy.
+
+## Local Development
+
+### 1. Database Migration
+Ensure you are at the monorepo root and apply the Prisma schema changes:
+```bash
+# Push schema changes to your local database
+npx prisma db push --schema=packages/backend/prisma/schema.prisma
+
+# OR if using migrations:
+npx prisma migrate dev --name init_opportunities --schema=packages/backend/prisma/schema.prisma
+
+# Generate Prisma client
+npx prisma generate --schema=packages/backend/prisma/schema.prisma
+```
+
+### 2. Start Backend
+```bash
+# Start the NestJS backend
+cd packages/backend
+npm run start:dev
+```
+The backend will run on `http://localhost:3001` (by default) and includes the new `opportunities` module.
+
+### 3. Start Next.js Client
+```bash
+# Start the Opportunities UI
+cd packages/opportunities
+pnpm install
+npm run dev
+```
+The application will run on `http://localhost:3003`. Next.js is configured via `next.config.ts` to proxy API requests to `http://localhost:3001`.
+
+### Integration Details
+- **Auth:** Uses the SSO cookies provided by `packages/backend/auth`. The proxy ensures credentials and cookies are passed to the NestJS backend automatically.
+- **UI:** Uses components from `@universe/ui` and tokens from `@universe/ui/vars.scss`.
diff --git a/packages/opportunities/app/Providers.tsx b/packages/opportunities/app/Providers.tsx

+'use client';
+
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+
+import { ThemeProvider } from './theme/ThemeContext';
+
+export function Providers({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
+  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    fetch('/api/auth/refresh', { method: 'POST' })
+      .then(res => {
+        if (res.ok) return res.json();
+        throw new Error('Refresh failed');
+      })
+      .then(data => {
+        if (data.access_token) {
+          localStorage.setItem('accessToken', data.access_token);
+        }
+      })
+      .catch(() => {
+        // Ignored, user is just a guest
+      })
+      .finally(() => {
+        setReady(true);
+      });
+  }, []);
+
+  if (!ready) return <div style={{ padding: '2rem', textAlign: 'center' }}>Загрузка сессии...</div>;
+
+  return <ThemeProvider>{children}</ThemeProvider>;
+}

+'use client';
+
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { useParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
+import { Button, Spinner, Tag, Modal } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import Link from 'next/link';
+import styles from './page.module.scss';
+import { parseJwt } from '../utils/jwt';
+
+export default function OpportunityDetailsPage() {
+  const { id } = useParams();
+  const router = useRouter();
+  const [opportunity, setOpportunity] = useState<any>(null);
+  const [applications, setApplications] = useState<any[]>([]);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [applying, setApplying] = useState(false);
+  const [currentUserId, setCurrentUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
+  const [isApplyModalOpen, setIsApplyModalOpen] = useState(false);
+  const [motivation, setMotivation] = useState('');
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!id) return;
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    let payload: any = null;
+    if (token) {
+      payload = parseJwt(token);
+      if (payload) setCurrentUserId(payload.sub);
+    }
+
+    fetch(`/api/opportunities/${id}`)
+      .then((res) => {
+        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Возможность не найдена');
+        return res.json();
+      })
+      .then((data) => {
+        setOpportunity(data);
+        if (token && payload?.sub === data.ownerId) {
+          fetch(`/api/opportunities/${id}/applications`, {
+            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
+          })
+            .then(res => res.json())
+            .then(apps => setApplications(Array.isArray(apps) ? apps : []));
+        }
+      })
+      .catch((err) => setError(err.message))
+      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
+  }, [id]);
+
+  const submitApplication = async () => {
+    if (!motivation.trim()) {
+      alert('Пожалуйста, напишите сопроводительное письмо');
+      return;
+    }
+    
+    setApplying(true);
+    try {
+      const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+      const res = await fetch(`/api/opportunities/${id}/apply`, {
+        method: 'POST',
+        headers: { 
+          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+          ...(token && { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` })
+        },
+        body: JSON.stringify({
+          applicantName: 'Текущий пользователь',
+          contactInfo: 'Скрыто',
+          motivation,
+        }),
+      });
+      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Не удалось откликнуться');
+      alert('Отклик успешно отправлен!');
+      setIsApplyModalOpen(false);
+      router.push('/my-applications');
+    } catch (err: any) {
+      alert(err.message);
+    } finally {
+      setApplying(false);
+    }
+  };
+
+  const changeStatus = async (status: string) => {
+    const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
+    await fetch(`/api/opportunities/${id}/status`, {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: { 
+        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ status })
+    });
+    setOpportunity((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, status }));
+  };
+
+  const updateAppStatus = async (appId: string, status: string) => {
+    const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
+    await fetch(`/api/opportunities/applications/${appId}/status`, {
+      method: 'PUT',
+      headers: { 
+        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ status })
+    });
+    setApplications(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === appId ? { ...a, status } : a));
+  };
+
+  if (loading) return <div className={styles.container} style={{textAlign: 'center'}}><Spinner size="large" /></div>;
+  if (error) return <div className={styles.container} style={{color: 'red', textAlign: 'center'}}>{error}</div>;
+  if (!opportunity) return <div className={styles.container} style={{textAlign: 'center'}}>Возможность не найдена</div>;
+
+  const isOwner = currentUserId === opportunity.ownerId;
+
+  return (
+    <div className={styles.container}>
+      <Link href="/">
+        <Button isTransparent={true} size="small" className={styles.backBtn}>&larr; Назад к списку</Button>
+      </Link>
+      
+      <div className={styles.header} style={{ marginBottom: '2rem', borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(var(--text-primary-rgb, 0, 0, 0), 0.1)', paddingBottom: '1rem' }}>
+        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '2.5rem', fontWeight: 800, margin: '0 0 1rem 0', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--text-primary), var(--text-secondary))', WebkitBackgroundClip: 'text', WebkitTextFillColor: 'transparent', lineHeight: 1.2 }}>{opportunity.title}</h1>
+        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '1rem', alignItems: 'center' }}>
+          <Tag color={opportunity.paymentType === 'PAID' ? 'green' : 'gray'}>
+            {opportunity.paymentType === 'PAID' ? 'Оплачиваемая' : 'Неоплачиваемая'}
+          </Tag>
+          <Tag color="blue">{opportunity.status}</Tag>
+          {opportunity.lifecycleState && <Tag color="purple">{opportunity.lifecycleState}</Tag>}
+        </div>
+      </div>
+
+      <div className={styles.meta}>
+        <span>От: {opportunity.owner?.name || opportunity.owner?.email || 'Неизвестно'}</span>
+        <span>•</span>
+        <span>Опубликовано: {new Date(opportunity.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}</span>
+      </div>
+
+      <div className={styles.descriptionBox}>
+        <h3 className={styles.descriptionTitle}>Описание проекта</h3>
+        <p className={styles.descriptionText}>{opportunity.description}</p>
+      </div>
+
+      {isOwner && (opportunity.status === 'DRAFT' || opportunity.status === 'REQUIRES_CHANGES') && (
+        <div style={{ marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
+          <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => changeStatus('READY_FOR_REVIEW')}>Отправить на проверку (Модерацию)</Button>
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      {isOwner && applications.length > 0 && (
+        <div style={{ marginTop: '3rem', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border-color)', paddingTop: '2rem' }}>
+          <h3>Отклики пользователей ({applications.length})</h3>
+          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '1rem' }}>
+            {applications.map(app => (
+              <div key={app.id} style={{ border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', padding: '1rem', borderRadius: '8px' }}>
+                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
+                  <strong>{app.applicant?.name || app.applicant?.email || 'Аноним'}</strong>
+                  <Tag color={app.status === 'ACCEPTED' ? 'green' : app.status === 'REJECTED' ? 'red' : 'orange'}>
+                    {app.status}
+                  </Tag>
+                </div>
+                <p style={{ margin: '0.5rem 0', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>{app.motivation}</p>
+                {app.status === 'SUBMITTED' || app.status === 'UNDER_REVIEW' ? (
+                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '1rem', marginTop: '1rem' }}>
+                    <Button size="small" variant="primary" onClick={() => updateAppStatus(app.id, 'ACCEPTED')}>Принять</Button>
+                    <Button size="small" isTransparent={true} onClick={() => updateAppStatus(app.id, 'REJECTED')}>Отклонить</Button>
+                  </div>
+                ) : null}
+              </div>
+            ))}
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      {!isOwner && opportunity.status === 'PUBLISHED' && (
+        <div className={styles.applySection}>
+          <h3 className={styles.applyTitle}>Заинтересовало?</h3>
+          <p className={styles.applySubtitle}>Отправьте отклик, чтобы связаться с владельцем проекта.</p>
+          <div>
+            <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setIsApplyModalOpen(true)}>
+              Откликнуться
+            </Button>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      <Modal
+        open={isApplyModalOpen}
+        onClose={() => setIsApplyModalOpen(false)}
+        title="Подача заявки"
+        width={500}
+      >
+        <p style={{ margin: '0 0 1rem 0' }}>Напишите сопроводительное письмо или оставьте комментарий для владельца проекта:</p>
+        <textarea
+          className={styles.textarea}
+          rows={5}
+          value={motivation}
+          onChange={(e) => setMotivation(e.target.value)}
+          placeholder="Почему вы подходите на эту роль?"
+          disabled={applying}
+        />
+        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '1rem', marginTop: '1.5rem', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
+          <Button isTransparent={true} onClick={() => setIsApplyModalOpen(false)} disabled={applying}>
+            Отмена
+          </Button>
+          <Button variant="primary" onClick={submitApplication} disabled={applying || !motivation.trim()}>
+            {applying ? 'Отправка...' : 'Отправить'}
+          </Button>
+        </div>
+      </Modal>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

diff --git a/packages/opportunities/app/admin/page.tsx b/packages/opportunities/app/admin/page.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..92cf0d9
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/opportunities/app/admin/page.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+'use client';
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Spinner, Button, Select } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import styles from './page.module.scss';
+
+export default function AdminPage() {
+  const [users, setUsers] = useState<any[]>([]);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    fetchUsers();
+  }, []);
+
+  const fetchUsers = async () => {
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    try {
+      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/users', {
+        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
+      });
+      if (res.ok) {
+        const data = await res.json();
+        setUsers(data);
+      }
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error(e);
+    } finally {
+      setLoading(false);
+    }
+  };
+
+  const handleRoleChange = async (id: string, role: string) => {
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    await fetch(`/api/admin/users/${id}/role`, {
+      method: 'PUT',
+      headers: {
+        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ role })
+    });
+    setUsers(prev => prev.map(u => u.id === id ? { ...u, role } : u));
+  };
+
+  if (loading) return <div className={styles.loading}><Spinner size="large" /></div>;
+
+  return (
+    <div className={styles.container}>
+      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
+        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '2rem', fontWeight: 800, margin: 0, background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--text-primary), var(--text-secondary))', WebkitBackgroundClip: 'text', WebkitTextFillColor: 'transparent' }}>Управление пользователями</h1>
+      </div>
+
+      {users.length === 0 ? (
+        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-disabled)', marginTop: '4rem' }}>Нет пользователей</div>
+      ) : (
+        users.map(u => (
+          <div key={u.id} className={styles.card}>
+            <div className={styles.info}>
+              <h3 className={styles.name}>{u.name || 'Без имени'}</h3>
+              <p className={styles.email}>{u.email}</p>
+            </div>
+            <div className={styles.actions}>
+              <Select
+                value={u.role}
+                onChange={(e) => handleRoleChange(u.id, e.target.value)}
+                options={[
+                  { value: 'USER', label: 'USER' },
+                  { value: 'STUDENT', label: 'STUDENT' },
+                  { value: 'MODERATOR', label: 'MODERATOR' },
+                  { value: 'ADMIN', label: 'ADMIN' }
+                ]}
+              />
+            </div>
+          </div>
+        ))
+      )}
+    </div>
+  );
+}

diff --git a/packages/opportunities/app/components/Navbar.tsx b/packages/opportunities/app/components/Navbar.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..f6f4b0a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/opportunities/app/components/Navbar.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,87 @@
+'use client';
+
+import Link from 'next/link';
+import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Button } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import { ThemeSwitcher } from '../theme/ThemeSwitcher';
+import { parseJwt } from '../utils/jwt';
+import { Bell } from 'lucide-react';
+import styles from './Navbar.module.scss';
+
+export function Navbar() {
+  const pathname = usePathname();
+  const [isLoggedIn, setIsLoggedIn] = useState(false);
+  const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<string | null>(null);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const checkAuth = () => {
+      const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+      setIsLoggedIn(!!token);
+      if (token) {
+        try {
+          const payload = parseJwt(token);
+          setUserRole(payload.role);
+        } catch (e) {
+          setUserRole(null);
+        }
+      } else {
+        setUserRole(null);
+      }
+    };
+    
+    checkAuth();
+    window.addEventListener('storage', checkAuth);
+    window.addEventListener('auth-change', checkAuth);
+    return () => {
+      window.removeEventListener('storage', checkAuth);
+      window.removeEventListener('auth-change', checkAuth);
+    };
+  }, [pathname]);
+
+  if (pathname === '/login') return null;
+
+  return (
+    <header className={styles.navbar}>
+      <div className={styles.container}>
+        <div className={styles.brand}>
+          <Link href="/" className={styles.logo}>
+            Платформа можливостей
+          </Link>
+        </div>
+
+        <nav className={styles.actions}>
+          {isLoggedIn ? (
+            <>
+              <Link href="/moderation" className={pathname === '/moderation' ? styles.activeLink : styles.link}>
+                Адмінка
+              </Link>
+              <Link href="/my-opportunities" className={pathname === '/my-opportunities' ? styles.activeLink : styles.link}>
+                Мої можливості
+              </Link>
+              <Link href="/my-applications" className={pathname === '/my-applications' ? styles.activeLink : styles.link}>
+                Мої відгуки
+              </Link>
+              <Link href="/notifications" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>
+                <Bell size={20} />
+              </Link>
+              <Link href="/create">
+                <Button variant="primary" size="small">Створити</Button>
+              </Link>
+              <Link href="/profile">
+                <div className={styles.avatar}>П</div>
+              </Link>
+            </>
+          ) : (
+            <Link href="/login">
+              <Button variant="primary" size="small">Увійти</Button>
+            </Link>
+          )}
+          <div className={styles.themeToggle}>
+            <ThemeSwitcher compact={true} showLabel={false} />
+          </div>
+        </nav>
+      </div>
+    </header>
+  );
+}

diff --git a/packages/opportunities/app/create/page.tsx b/packages/opportunities/app/create/page.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8903e7a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/opportunities/app/create/page.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,130 @@
+'use client';
+
+import { useState } from 'react';
+import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
+import { Button, TextInput, Select } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import styles from './page.module.scss';
+
+export default function CreateOpportunityPage() {
+  const router = useRouter();
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+
+  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
+    title: '',
+    description: '',
+    ownerContactInfo: '',
+    paymentType: 'UNPAID',
+    paymentDetails: '',
+  });
+
+  const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
+    setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [e.target.name]: e.target.value }));
+  };
+
+  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
+    e.preventDefault();
+    setLoading(true);
+    setError('');
+
+    try {
+      const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+      const res = await fetch('/api/opportunities', {
+        method: 'POST',
+        headers: { 
+          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+          ...(token && { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` })
+        },
+        body: JSON.stringify(formData),
+      });
+
+      if (!res.ok) {
+        throw new Error('Ошибка при создании возможности');
+      }
+
+      router.push('/my-opportunities');
+    } catch (err: any) {
+      setError(err.message || 'Произошла ошибка');
+    } finally {
+      setLoading(false);
+    }
+  };
+
+  return (
+    <div className={styles.container}>
+      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
+        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '2rem', fontWeight: 800, margin: 0, background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--text-primary), var(--text-secondary))', WebkitBackgroundClip: 'text', WebkitTextFillColor: 'transparent' }}>Створити можливість</h1>
+      </div>
+      
+      {error && <div className={styles.error}>{error}</div>}
+
+      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className={styles.form}>
+        <div className={styles.field}>
+          <label className={styles.label}>Название *</label>
+          <TextInput
+            name="title"
+            value={formData.title}
+            onChange={handleChange}
+            placeholder="Например: Frontend-разработчик на проект X"
+            required
+          />
+        </div>
+
+        <div className={styles.field}>
+          <label className={styles.label}>Описание *</label>
+          <textarea
+            name="description"
+            value={formData.description}
+            onChange={handleChange}
+            placeholder="Опишите суть проекта и требования..."
+            rows={5}
+            className={styles.textarea}
+            required
+          />
+        </div>
+
+        <div className={styles.field}>
+          <label className={styles.label}>Контактная информация *</label>
+          <TextInput
+            name="ownerContactInfo"
+            value={formData.ownerContactInfo}
+            onChange={handleChange}
+            placeholder="Email или Telegram (@username)"
+            required
+          />
+        </div>
+
+        <div className={styles.field}>
+          <label className={styles.label}>Тип оплаты</label>
+          <Select
+            value={formData.paymentType}
+            onChange={(val) => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, paymentType: val }))}
+            options={[
+              { value: 'UNPAID', label: 'Неоплачиваемая' },
+              { value: 'PAID', label: 'Оплачиваемая' }
+            ]}
+          />
+        </div>
+
+        {formData.paymentType === 'PAID' && (
+          <div className={styles.field}>
+            <label className={styles.label}>Детали оплаты</label>
+            <TextInput
+              name="paymentDetails"
+              value={formData.paymentDetails}
+              onChange={handleChange}
+              placeholder="Например: 500$ за проект"
+            />
+          </div>
+        )}
+
+        <div className={styles.actions}>
+          <Button type="button" isTransparent={true} onClick={() => router.back()}>Отмена</Button>
+          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={loading}>
+            {loading ? 'Отправка...' : 'Отправить на модерацию'}
+          </Button>
+        </div>
+      </form>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

+import type { Metadata } from 'next';
+import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
+import { Geist, Geist_Mono } from 'next/font/google';
+import './globals.css';
+import '@universe/ui/vars.scss';
+
+const geistSans = Geist({
+  variable: '--font-geist-sans',
+  subsets: ['latin'],
+});
+
+const geistMono = Geist_Mono({
+  variable: '--font-geist-mono',
+  subsets: ['latin'],
+});
+
+export const metadata: Metadata = {
+  title: 'Opportunities | Karazin UniVerse',
+  description: 'Платформа «Возможности» (Opportunities board)',
+};
+
+import { Providers } from './Providers';
+import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar';
+
+export default function RootLayout({ children }: Readonly<{ children: ReactNode }>) {
+  return (
+    <html
+      lang="uk"
+      suppressHydrationWarning
+      className={`${geistSans.variable} ${geistMono.variable}`}
+    >
+      <body>
+        <Providers>
+          <Navbar />
+          <main className="animate-fade-in">{children}</main>
+        </Providers>
+      </body>
+    </html>
+  );

+'use client';
+
+import { useState } from 'react';
+import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
+import { Button, TextInput } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import { User, Lock, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';
+import Link from 'next/link';
+import styles from './page.module.scss';
+import { ThemeSwitcher } from '../theme/ThemeSwitcher';
+
+export default function LoginPage() {
+  const router = useRouter();
+  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
+  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
+  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
+
+  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
+    e.preventDefault();
+    setLoading(true);
+    setError('');
+
+    try {
+      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
+        method: 'POST',
+        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
+      });
+
+      if (!res.ok) {
+        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
+        throw new Error(data.message || 'Ошибка авторизации');
+      }
+
+      const data = await res.json();
+      if (data.access_token) {
+        localStorage.setItem('accessToken', data.access_token);
+        window.location.href = '/profile';
+      } else {
+        throw new Error('Токен не получен');
+      }
+    } catch (err: any) {
+      setError(err.message);
+    } finally {
+      setLoading(false);
+    }
+  };
+
+  return (
+    <div className={styles.page}>
+      <div className={styles.themeBar}>
+        <ThemeSwitcher compact={true} />
+      </div>
+      <div className={styles.center}>
+        <form className={styles.card} onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
+          <div className={styles.brand}>
+            <h1>Вхід в систему</h1>
+            <p>Платформа можливостей</p>
+          </div>
+
+          <div className={styles.googleSection}>
+            {/* Mock Google Login Button just for visual parity with uniHub */}
+            <Button
+              type="button"
+              variant="secondary"
+              size="large"
+              style={{ width: '100%', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px' }}
+            >
+              <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
+                <path d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z" fill="#4285F4" />
+                <path d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z" fill="#34A853" />
+                <path d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.07H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.93l2.85-2.22.81-.62z" fill="#FBBC05" />
+                <path d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.07l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.53 6.16-4.53z" fill="#EA4335" />
+                <path d="M1 1h22v22H1z" fill="none" />
+              </svg>
+              Вхід через Google
+            </Button>
+          </div>
+
+          <div className={styles.divider}>
+            <span>Або через Moodle</span>
+          </div>
+
+          <div className={styles.inputGroup}>
+            <label htmlFor="email">Email або логін</label>
+            <div className={styles.inputWrap}>
+              <span className={styles.icon}><User size={16} /></span>
+              <TextInput
+                id="email"
+                type="text"
+                size="large"
+                value={email}
+                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
+                required
+                placeholder="Введіть email або логін..."
+                className={styles.inputElement}
+              />
+            </div>
+          </div>
+
+          <div className={styles.inputGroup}>
+            <label htmlFor="password">Пароль</label>
+            <div className={styles.inputWrap}>
+              <span className={styles.icon}><Lock size={16} /></span>
+              <TextInput
+                id="password"
+                type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
+                size="large"
+                value={password}
+                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
+                required
+                placeholder="Введіть пароль..."
+                className={styles.inputElementRight}
+              />
+              <button
+                type="button"
+                className={styles.passwordToggle}
+                onClick={() => setShowPassword((prev) => !prev)}
+              >
+                {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={16} /> : <Eye size={16} />}
+              </button>
+            </div>
+          </div>
+
+          {error && <p className={styles.error}>{error}</p>}
+
+          <div style={{ marginTop: '0.5rem' }}>
+            <Button type="submit" variant="primary" size="large" disabled={loading} className={styles.submit}>
+              {loading ? 'Завантаження...' : 'Увійти'}
+            </Button>
+          </div>
+
+          <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '0.5rem' }}>
+            <Link href="/" style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: '0.875rem' }}>
+              &larr; Повернутися на головну
+            </Link>
+          </div>
+        </form>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

diff --git a/packages/opportunities/app/moderation/page.tsx b/packages/opportunities/app/moderation/page.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..66524a1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/opportunities/app/moderation/page.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,130 @@
+'use client';
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Button, Spinner, Modal } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import styles from './page.module.scss';
+import Link from 'next/link';
+
+export default function ModerationPage() {
+  const [items, setItems] = useState<any[]>([]);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+  const [rejectModal, setRejectModal] = useState<{ open: boolean, id: string | null, comment: string }>({ open: false, id: null, comment: '' });
+  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    fetchItems();
+  }, []);
+
+  const fetchItems = () => {
+    setLoading(true);
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    fetch('/api/opportunities?status=READY_FOR_REVIEW', {
+      headers: { ...(token && { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }) }
+    })
+      .then((res) => res.json())
+      .then((data) => {
+        if (Array.isArray(data)) setItems(data);
+        else setItems([]);
+      })
+      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
+  };
+
+  const handleModerate = async (id: string, action: string, comment?: string) => {
+    setSubmitting(true);
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    await fetch(`/api/opportunities/${id}/moderate`, {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: { 
+        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+        ...(token && { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` })
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ action, comment }),
+    });
+    setItems((prev) => prev.filter((i) => i.id !== id));
+    setSubmitting(false);
+  };
+
+  const handleOpenReject = (id: string) => {
+    setRejectModal({ open: true, id, comment: '' });
+  };
+
+  const submitRejectWithComment = async () => {
+    if (rejectModal.id) {
+      // Actually REQUIRE_CHANGES is better for comments, but user said "Reject with comment". 
+      // I'll send REJECT with comment.
+      await handleModerate(rejectModal.id, 'REJECT', rejectModal.comment);
+      setRejectModal({ open: false, id: null, comment: '' });
+    }
+  };
+
+  if (loading) {
+    return <div className={styles.loading}><Spinner size="large" /></div>;
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className={styles.container}>
+      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
+        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '2rem', fontWeight: 800, margin: 0, background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--text-primary), var(--text-secondary))', WebkitBackgroundClip: 'text', WebkitTextFillColor: 'transparent' }}>Адмінпанель (Модерація)</h1>
+      </div>
+      
+      {items.length === 0 ? (
+        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-disabled)', marginTop: '4rem' }}>
+          <h3>Немає нових можливостей для перевірки.</h3>
+        </div>
+      ) : (
+        items.map((item) => (
+          <div key={item.id} className={styles.card}>
+            <Link href={`/${item.id}`} style={{ textDecoration: 'none' }}>
+              <h3 className={styles.cardTitle}>{item.title}</h3>
+            </Link>
+            <p className={styles.description}>{item.description}</p>
+            <div className={styles.actions}>
+              <Button onClick={() => handleModerate(item.id, 'APPROVE')} variant="primary" style={{ backgroundColor: 'var(--chart-success)', borderColor: 'var(--chart-success)' }}>
+                Схвалити
+              </Button>
+              <Button isTransparent={true} onClick={() => handleModerate(item.id, 'REJECT')}>
+                Відхилити
+              </Button>
+              <Button isTransparent={true} onClick={() => handleOpenReject(item.id)} style={{ color: 'var(--chart-danger)' }}>
+                Відхилити з коментарем
+              </Button>
+            </div>
+          </div>
+        ))
+      )}
+
+      <Modal
+        open={rejectModal.open}
+        onClose={() => setRejectModal({ ...rejectModal, open: false })}
+        title="Відхилення з коментарем"
+        width={500}
+      >
+        <p style={{ margin: '0 0 1rem 0' }}>Напишіть причину відхилення (буде надіслано автору):</p>
+        <textarea
+          rows={5}
+          value={rejectModal.comment}
+          onChange={(e) => setRejectModal({ ...rejectModal, comment: e.target.value })}
+          placeholder="Ваша заявка відхилена, тому що..."
+          disabled={submitting}
+          style={{
+            width: '100%',
+            padding: '1rem',
+            border: '1px solid rgba(var(--text-primary-rgb, 0, 0, 0), 0.15)',
+            borderRadius: '8px',
+            fontFamily: 'inherit',
+            backgroundColor: 'var(--bg-surface)',
+            color: 'var(--text-primary)',
+            resize: 'vertical',
+          }}
+        />
+        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '1rem', marginTop: '1.5rem', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
+          <Button isTransparent={true} onClick={() => setRejectModal({ ...rejectModal, open: false })} disabled={submitting}>
+            Скасувати
+          </Button>
+          <Button variant="primary" onClick={submitRejectWithComment} disabled={submitting || !rejectModal.comment.trim()} style={{ backgroundColor: 'var(--chart-danger)', borderColor: 'var(--chart-danger)' }}>
+            {submitting ? 'Відправка...' : 'Відхилити'}
+          </Button>
+        </div>
+      </Modal>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

diff --git a/packages/opportunities/app/my-applications/page.tsx b/packages/opportunities/app/my-applications/page.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..5ef8d8d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/opportunities/app/my-applications/page.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,103 @@
+'use client';
+
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Button, Tag, Spinner, Empty } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import Link from 'next/link';
+import styles from './page.module.scss';
+
+interface Application {
+  id: string;
+  opportunity: { id: string, title: string };
+  status: string;
+  createdAt: string;
+  ownerComment?: string;
+}
+
+export default function MyApplicationsPage() {
+  const [applications, setApplications] = useState<Application[]>([]);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    fetch('/api/opportunities/my/applications', {
+      headers: { ...(token && { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }) }
+    })
+      .then((res) => res.json())
+      .then((data) => {
+        if (Array.isArray(data)) setApplications(data);
+        else setApplications([]);
+      })
+      .catch((err) => console.error(err))
+      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
+  }, []);
+
+  const getStatusTag = (status: string) => {
+    switch(status) {
+      case 'SUBMITTED': return <Tag color="blue">Отправлен</Tag>;
+      case 'UNDER_REVIEW': return <Tag color="orange">На рассмотрении</Tag>;
+      case 'ACCEPTED': return <Tag color="green">Принят</Tag>;
+      case 'REJECTED': return <Tag color="red">Отклонен</Tag>;
+      case 'WITHDRAWN': return <Tag color="gray">Отозван</Tag>;
+      default: return <Tag color="gray">{status}</Tag>;
+    }
+  };
+
+  return (
+    <div className={styles.container}>
+      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
+        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '2rem', fontWeight: 800, margin: 0, background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--text-primary), var(--text-secondary))', WebkitBackgroundClip: 'text', WebkitTextFillColor: 'transparent' }}>Мої відгуки</h1>
+      </div>
+
+      {loading ? (
+        <div className={styles.loading}>
+          <Spinner size="large" />
+        </div>
+      ) : applications.length === 0 ? (
+        <Empty description="Вы еще не отправляли отклики" />
+      ) : (
+        <div className={styles.list}>
+          {applications.map((app) => (
+            <div key={app.id} className={styles.card}>
+              <div>
+                <h3 className={styles.cardTitle}>{app.opportunity?.title || 'Возможность удалена'}</h3>
+                <div className={styles.meta}>
+                  {getStatusTag(app.status)}
+                  <span className={styles.date}>
+                    Дата отклика: {new Date(app.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}
+                  </span>
+                </div>
+                {app.ownerComment && (
+                  <p className={styles.comment}>
+                    <strong>Комментарий автора:</strong> {app.ownerComment}
+                  </p>
+                )}
+              </div>
+              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '0.5rem', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
+                <Link href={`/${app.opportunity?.id}`}>
+                  <Button variant="secondary" size="small">Перейти к возможности</Button>
+                </Link>
+                {app.status === 'SUBMITTED' || app.status === 'UNDER_REVIEW' ? (
+                  <Button 
+                    isTransparent={true} 
+                    size="small" 
+                    onClick={async () => {
+                      const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+                      await fetch(`/api/opportunities/my/applications/${app.id}/withdraw`, {
+                        method: 'PUT',
+                        headers: { ...(token && { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }) }
+                      });
+                      setApplications(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === app.id ? { ...a, status: 'WITHDRAWN' } : a));
+                    }}
+                    style={{ color: 'var(--chart-danger)' }}
+                  >
+                    Отозвать
+                  </Button>
+                ) : null}
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          ))}
+        </div>
+      )}
+    </div>
+  );
+}

diff --git a/packages/opportunities/app/my-opportunities/[id]/page.tsx b/packages/opportunities/app/my-opportunities/[id]/page.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..808feb2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/opportunities/app/my-opportunities/[id]/page.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,118 @@
+'use client';
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { useParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
+import { Spinner, Button, Select } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import styles from './page.module.scss';
+
+export default function OwnerOpportunityPage() {
+  const { id } = useParams();
+  const router = useRouter();
+  const [opportunity, setOpportunity] = useState<any>(null);
+  const [applications, setApplications] = useState<any[]>([]);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    fetchData();
+  }, [id]);
+
+  const fetchData = async () => {
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    try {
+      const oppRes = await fetch(`/api/opportunities/${id}`);
+      if (oppRes.ok) {
+        setOpportunity(await oppRes.json());
+      }
+
+      if (token) {
+        const appsRes = await fetch(`/api/opportunities/${id}/applications`, {
+          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
+        });
+        if (appsRes.ok) {
+          setApplications(await appsRes.json());
+        }
+      }
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error(e);
+    } finally {
+      setLoading(false);
+    }
+  };
+
+  const handleAppStatus = async (appId: string, status: string) => {
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    await fetch(`/api/opportunities/${id}/applications/${appId}/status`, {
+      method: 'PUT',
+      headers: {
+        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ status })
+    });
+    fetchData();
+  };
+
+  const handleLifecycle = async (lifecycleState: string) => {
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    await fetch(`/api/opportunities/${id}/lifecycle`, {
+      method: 'PUT',
+      headers: {
+        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ lifecycleState })
+    });
+    fetchData();
+  };
+
+  if (loading) return <div className={styles.loading}><Spinner size="large" /></div>;
+  if (!opportunity) return <div className={styles.loading}>Не найдено</div>;
+
+  return (
+    <div className={styles.container}>
+      <Button isTransparent={true} onClick={() => router.back()} style={{ marginBottom: '1rem' }}>
+        ← Назад
+      </Button>
+      <div className={styles.card}>
+        <h1 className={styles.title}>{opportunity.title}</h1>
+        <p className={styles.description}>{opportunity.description}</p>
+        
+        <div style={{ marginTop: '2rem', display: 'flex', gap: '1rem', alignItems: 'center' }}>
+          <strong>Статус модерации:</strong> {opportunity.status}
+          <strong style={{ marginLeft: '1rem' }}>Жизненный цикл:</strong>
+          <Select
+            value={opportunity.lifecycleState}
+            onChange={(e) => handleLifecycle(e.target.value)}
+            options={[
+              { value: 'START', label: 'Старт' },
+              { value: 'ACTIVE', label: 'Активная фаза' },
+              { value: 'PAUSED', label: 'На паузе' },
+              { value: 'COMPLETED', label: 'Завершена' },
+              { value: 'CANCELLED', label: 'Отменена' },
+            ]}
+          />
+        </div>
+      </div>
+
+      <div className={styles.card}>
+        <h2 className={styles.sectionTitle}>Отклики ({applications.length})</h2>
+        {applications.length === 0 ? (
+          <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-disabled)' }}>Пока нет откликов</p>
+        ) : (
+          applications.map(app => (
+            <div key={app.id} className={styles.applicationCard}>
+              <div>
+                <p className={styles.appName}>{app.applicantName} ({app.contactInfo})</p>
+                <p className={styles.appMotivation}>{app.motivation}</p>
+                <p style={{ margin: '0.25rem 0 0 0', fontSize: '0.85rem', color: 'var(--accent-primary)' }}>Статус: {app.status}</p>
+              </div>
+              <div className={styles.actions}>
+                <Button variant="primary" size="small" onClick={() => handleAppStatus(app.id, 'ACCEPTED')} style={{ backgroundColor: 'var(--chart-success)', borderColor: 'var(--chart-success)' }}>Принять</Button>
+                <Button isTransparent={true} size="small" onClick={() => handleAppStatus(app.id, 'REJECTED')} style={{ color: 'var(--chart-danger)' }}>Отклонить</Button>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          ))
+        )}
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

diff --git a/packages/opportunities/app/my-opportunities/page.tsx b/packages/opportunities/app/my-opportunities/page.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b31d720
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/opportunities/app/my-opportunities/page.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,84 @@
+'use client';
+
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Button, Tag, Spinner, Empty } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import Link from 'next/link';
+import styles from './page.module.scss';
+
+interface Opportunity {
+  id: string;
+  title: string;
+  status: string;
+  createdAt: string;
+  moderationComment?: string;
+}
+
+export default function MyOpportunitiesPage() {
+  const [opportunities, setOpportunities] = useState<Opportunity[]>([]);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    fetch('/api/opportunities/my', {
+      headers: { ...(token && { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }) }
+    })
+      .then((res) => res.json())
+      .then((data) => {
+        if (Array.isArray(data)) setOpportunities(data);
+        else setOpportunities([]);
+      })
+      .catch((err) => console.error(err))
+      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
+  }, []);
+
+  const getStatusTag = (status: string) => {
+    switch(status) {
+      case 'DRAFT': return <Tag color="gray">Черновик</Tag>;
+      case 'READY_FOR_REVIEW': return <Tag color="blue">На модерации</Tag>;
+      case 'REQUIRES_CHANGES': return <Tag color="orange">Требует правок</Tag>;
+      case 'PUBLISHED': return <Tag color="green">Опубликована</Tag>;
+      case 'REJECTED': return <Tag color="red">Отклонена</Tag>;
+      default: return <Tag color="gray">{status}</Tag>;
+    }
+  };
+
+  return (
+    <div className={styles.container}>
+      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
+        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '2rem', fontWeight: 800, margin: 0, background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--text-primary), var(--text-secondary))', WebkitBackgroundClip: 'text', WebkitTextFillColor: 'transparent' }}>Мої можливості</h1>
+      </div>
+
+      {loading ? (
+        <div className={styles.loading}>
+          <Spinner size="large" />
+        </div>
+      ) : opportunities.length === 0 ? (
+        <Empty description="Вы еще не создали ни одной возможности" />
+      ) : (
+        <div className={styles.list}>
+          {opportunities.map((opp) => (
+            <div key={opp.id} className={styles.card}>
+              <div>
+                <h3 className={styles.cardTitle}>{opp.title}</h3>
+                <div className={styles.meta}>
+                  {getStatusTag(opp.status)}
+                  <span className={styles.date}>
+                    Создано: {new Date(opp.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}
+                  </span>
+                </div>
+                {opp.moderationComment && (
+                  <p className={styles.comment}>
+                    <strong>Комментарий модератора:</strong> {opp.moderationComment}
+                  </p>
+                )}
+              </div>
+              <Link href={`/my-opportunities/${opp.id}`}>
+                <Button variant="secondary" size="small">Управление</Button>
+              </Link>
+            </div>
+          ))}
+        </div>
+      )}
+    </div>
+  );
+}

diff --git a/packages/opportunities/app/notifications/page.tsx b/packages/opportunities/app/notifications/page.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ac76712
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/opportunities/app/notifications/page.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,99 @@
+'use client';
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Spinner } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import Link from 'next/link';
+import styles from './page.module.scss';
+
+export default function NotificationsPage() {
+  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<any[]>([]);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    fetchNotifications();
+  }, []);
+
+  const fetchNotifications = async () => {
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    if (!token) {
+      setLoading(false);
+      return;
+    }
+    try {
+      const res = await fetch('/api/notifications', {
+        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
+      });
+      const data = await res.json();
+      if (Array.isArray(data)) setNotifications(data);
+    } catch (e) {
+      console.error(e);
+    } finally {
+      setLoading(false);
+    }
+  };
+
+  const markAsRead = async (id: string, e?: React.MouseEvent) => {
+    if (e) e.preventDefault();
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    await fetch(`/api/notifications/${id}/read`, {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
+    });
+    setNotifications(prev => prev.map(n => n.id === id ? { ...n, isRead: true } : n));
+  };
+
+  const markAllAsRead = async () => {
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    await fetch(`/api/notifications/read-all`, {
+      method: 'POST',
+      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
+    });
+    setNotifications(prev => prev.map(n => ({ ...n, isRead: true })));
+  };
+
+  if (loading) return <div className={styles.loading}><Spinner size="large" /></div>;
+
+  return (
+    <div className={styles.container}>
+      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
+        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '2rem', fontWeight: 800, margin: 0, background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--text-primary), var(--text-secondary))', WebkitBackgroundClip: 'text', WebkitTextFillColor: 'transparent' }}>Уведомления</h1>
+        {notifications.some(n => !n.isRead) && (
+          <button onClick={markAllAsRead} style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', color: 'var(--accent-primary)', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '0.9rem', fontWeight: 500 }}>
+            Отметить все как прочитанные
+          </button>
+        )}
+      </div>
+
+      {notifications.length === 0 ? (
+        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-disabled)', marginTop: '4rem' }}>Нет новых уведомлений</div>
+      ) : (
+        notifications.map(n => {
+          const content = (
+            <>
+              <h3 className={styles.title}>{n.title}</h3>
+              <p className={styles.message}>{n.message}</p>
+              <span className={styles.date}>{new Date(n.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</span>
+            </>
+          );
+          return (
+            <div key={n.id} className={`${styles.card} ${!n.isRead ? styles.unread : ''}`}>
+              {n.link ? (
+                <Link href={n.link} className={styles.link} onClick={() => markAsRead(n.id)}>
+                  {content}
+                </Link>
+              ) : (
+                <div className={styles.link}>{content}</div>
+              )}
+              {!n.isRead && (
+                <div className={styles.actions}>
+                  <button onClick={(e) => markAsRead(n.id, e)} style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', cursor: 'pointer' }}>
+                    ✕
+                  </button>
+                </div>
+              )}
+            </div>
+          );
+        })
+      )}
+    </div>
+  );
+}

+  color: var(--text-secondary);
+}

+'use client';
+
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Button, Tag, Spinner, Empty, TextInput, Select } from '@universe/ui/una';
+import Link from 'next/link';
+import styles from './page.module.scss';
+
+interface Opportunity {
+  id: string;
+  title: string;
+  description: string;
+  status: string;
+  paymentType: string;
+  owner: { name: string; email: string };
+  createdAt: string;
+}
+
+export default function OpportunitiesPage() {
+  const [opportunities, setOpportunities] = useState<Opportunity[]>([]);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
+  const [paymentType, setPaymentType] = useState('');
+
+  const [isLoggedIn, setIsLoggedIn] = useState(false);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    let url = '/api/opportunities';
+    const params = new URLSearchParams();
+    if (search) params.append('search', search);
+    if (paymentType) params.append('paymentType', paymentType);
+    if (params.toString()) url += `?${params.toString()}`;
+
+    const token = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('accessToken') : null;
+    setIsLoggedIn(!!token);
+
+    fetch(url, {
+      headers: { ...(token && { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }) }
+    })
+      .then((res) => res.json())
+      .then((data) => {
+        if (Array.isArray(data)) setOpportunities(data);
+        else setOpportunities([]);
+      })
+      .catch((err) => console.error(err))
+      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
+  }, [search, paymentType]);
+
+  return (
+    <div className={styles.container}>
+      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
+        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '2rem', fontWeight: 800, margin: 0, background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--text-primary), var(--text-secondary))', WebkitBackgroundClip: 'text', WebkitTextFillColor: 'transparent' }}>Знайти можливості</h1>
+      </div>
+
+      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '1rem', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>
+        <TextInput 
+          placeholder="Поиск по названию или описанию..." 
+          value={search} 
+          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
+          style={{ flex: 1 }}
+        />
+        <Select 
+          value={paymentType} 
+          onChange={(val) => setPaymentType(val)}
+          options={[
+            { value: '', label: 'Все типы' },
+            { value: 'PAID', label: 'Оплачиваемые' },
+            { value: 'UNPAID', label: 'Неоплачиваемые' }
+          ]}
+        />
+      </div>
+
+      {loading ? (
+        <div className={styles.loading}>
+          <Spinner size="large" />
+        </div>
+      ) : opportunities.length === 0 ? (
+        <Empty description="Пока нет доступных возможностей" />
+      ) : (
+        <div className={styles.grid}>
+          {opportunities.map((opp) => (
+            <div key={opp.id} className={styles.card}>
+              <div className={styles.cardHeader}>
+                <h3 className={styles.cardTitle}>{opp.title}</h3>
+                <Tag color={opp.paymentType === 'PAID' ? 'green' : 'gray'}>
+                  {opp.paymentType === 'PAID' ? 'Оплачиваемая' : 'Неоплачиваемая'}
+                </Tag>
+              </div>
+              
+              <p className={styles.description}>
+                {opp.description}
+              </p>
+
+              <div className={styles.cardFooter}>
+                <span className={styles.author}>
+                  От: {opp.owner?.name || opp.owner?.email || 'Неизвестно'}
+                </span>
+                <Link href={`/${opp.id}`}>
+                  <Button isTransparent={true} size="small">Подробнее</Button>
+                </Link>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          ))}
+        </div>
+      )}
+    </div>
+  );
+}
